    57: // The three records after the protective entry must be entirely zero; the scan
    58: // stops at the first non-zero one because that is already the whole answer.
    59: BUSTER_LOCAL bool verify_disk_reserved_partitions_zeroed(ProtectiveMBR const* mbr)
    60: {
    61:     MBRPartitionRecord zero_record;
    62:     memset(&zero_record, 0, sizeof(zero_record));
    63: 
    64:     bool result = true;
    65:     for (int i = 1; i < 4 && result; i += 1)
    66:     {
    67:         result = memcmp(&mbr->partition_records[i], &zero_record, sizeof(zero_record)) == 0;
    68:     }
    69: 
    70:     return result;
    71: }
    72: 
    73: // Reports the first thing wrong with the image. The checks are ordered so that
    74: // each one only runs once its predecessors have vouched for the bytes it reads:
    75: // the sector-size check guards the MBR cast, and the MBR checks guard the
    76: // protective partition record.
    77: BUSTER_LOCAL VerificationError verify_disk(u8* disk, u64 disk_size)
    78: {
    79:     VerificationError result = VERIFICATION_ERROR_DISK_SUCCESS;
    80:     if (disk_size == 0)
    81:     {
    82:         result = VERIFICATION_ERROR_DISK_EMPTY;
    83:     }
    84:     else if (disk_size % 512 != 0)
    85:     {
    86:         result = VERIFICATION_ERROR_DISK_NOT_SECTOR_SIZED;
    87:     }
    88:     else
    89:     {
    90:         let mbr = (ProtectiveMBR*)disk;
    91:         let gpt_partition_record = &mbr->partition_records[0];
    92: 
    93:         if (mbr->unique_mbr_disk_signature != 0)
    94:         {
    95:             result = VERIFICATION_ERROR_MBR_DISK_SIGNATURE_NOT_ZERO;
    96:         }
    97:         else if (mbr->unknown != 0)
    98:         {
    99:             result = VERIFICATION_ERROR_UNKNOWN_NOT_ZERO;
   100:         }
   101:         else if (!verify_disk_reserved_partitions_zeroed(mbr))
   102:         {
   103:             result = VERIFICATION_ERROR_MBR_ZERO_PARTITIONS_NOT_ZEROED;

   209: BUSTER_LOCAL u32 crc32_compute(u8* p, u64 bytelength)
   210: {
   211:     u32 crc = 0xffffffff;
   212:     while (bytelength-- != 0)
   213:         crc = crc32_table[((u8)crc ^ *(p++))] ^ (crc >> 8);
   214:     // return (~crc); also works
   215:     return (crc ^ 0xffffffff);
   216: }
   217: 
   218: // BUSTER_LOCAL void crc32_fill(u32 *table)

   220: //     u8 index=0;
   221: //
   222: //     do
   223: //     {
   224: //         table[index]= index;
   225: //
   226: //         for (u8 z = 8; z; z--)
   227: //         {
   228: //             table[index] = (table[index] & 1) ? (table[index] >> 1) ^ 0xEDB88320 : table[index] >> 1;
   229: //         }
   230: //     }while(++index);
   231: // }

   233: STRUCT(GPTPartitionEntry)
   234: {
   235:     u8 partition_type_guid[16];
   236:     u8 unique_partition_guid[16];
   237:     u64 starting_lba;
   238:     u64 ending_lba;
   239:     u64 attributes;

   241: };

   243: static_assert(sizeof(GPTPartitionEntry) == 128);
   244: 
   245: int main()
   246: {
   247:     let arena = arena_create((ArenaCreation){});
   248:     let minimal_gpt = file_read(arena, S("build/minimal_fat32.img"), (FileReadOptions){});
   249:     let minimal = (u8*)minimal_gpt.pointer;
   250:     u64 sector_size = 512;
   251: 
   252:     if (minimal_gpt.length)
   253:         BUSTER_CHECK(minimal_gpt.length % sector_size == 0);
   254: 
   255:     u32 gpt_partition_entry_count = 128;
   256:     u64 expected_size = BUSTER_MB(64);
   257: 
   258:     let mine = (u8*)arena_allocate_bytes(arena, expected_size, sector_size);
   259:     let w = file_writer_init(mine, minimal_gpt.length);
   260:     let writer = &w;
   261: 
   262:     u64 partition_record_count = 4;
   263:     file_pad(writer, sector_size - (partition_record_count * sizeof(MBRPartitionRecord) + 2));
   264:     let mbr_partition_records = file_allocate(writer, MBRPartitionRecord, partition_record_count);
   265:     let size_in_lba_u64_minus_1 = expected_size / sector_size - 1;
   266:     mbr_partition_records[0] = (MBRPartitionRecord){
   267:         .boot_indicator = 0,
   268:         .starting_chs = sector_size,
   269:         .os_type = 0xee,
   270:         .ending_chs = 0xffffff,
   271:         .starting_lba = 1,
   272:         .size_in_lba = size_in_lba_u64_minus_1 == (u64)(u32)size_in_lba_u64_minus_1 ? (u32)size_in_lba_u64_minus_1 : UINT32_MAX,
   273:     };
   274:     file_write_byte(writer, 0x55);
   275:     file_write_byte(writer, 0xaa);
   276: 
   277:     let gpt_header = file_allocate(writer, GPTHeader, 1);
   278:     u64 first_usable_lba = 0x22;
   279:     *gpt_header = (GPTHeader){
   280:         .signature = efi_part,
   281:         .revision = 0x10000,
   282:         .header_size = gpt_header_size,
   283:         .header_crc32 = 0,
   284:         .reserved0 = 0,
   285:         .header_lba = 1,
   286:         .alternate_lba = size_in_lba_u64_minus_1,
   287:         .first_usable_lba = first_usable_lba,
   288:         .last_usable_lba = size_in_lba_u64_minus_1 - first_usable_lba + 1,
   289:         .partition_entry_lba = 2,
   290:         .partition_entry_count = gpt_partition_entry_count,
   291:         .partition_entry_size = sizeof(GPTPartitionEntry),
   292:     };
   293:     u8 guid[] = {
   294:         0xEC, 0x5C, 0x76, 0xEE, 0x47, 0x02, 0xCE, 0x4F, 0x90, 0xAA, 0x83, 0x5D, 0x69, 0xE8, 0xB0, 0xE0,
   295:     };
   296:     memcpy(gpt_header->disk_guid, guid, sizeof(guid));
   297: 
   298:     file_align(writer, sector_size);

   188: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CPunctuator c_token_shape_punctuator(CTokenShape shape)
   189: {
   190:     return c_token_shape_is_punctuator(shape) ? (CPunctuator)(shape & C_TOKEN_SHAPE_PUNCTUATOR_MASK) : C_PUNCTUATOR_NONE;
   191: }
   192: 
   193: // A set of punctuators as a 64-bit mask: bit `p` is a member test for
   194: // CPunctuator `p`.  Every id is a valid bit position because the whole enum
   195: // fits in 64 values, which the check below holds it to; adding a 65th
   196: // punctuator fails the build rather than silently truncating a set.
   197: // C_PUNCTUATOR_NONE is bit 0, so a set that leaves bit 0 clear answers "is
   198: // this token one of these punctuators" with no kind test — the same
   199: // invariant c_token_is_punctuator relies on.
   200: #define C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(punctuator) ((u64)1 << (punctuator))
   201: BUSTER_CT_CHECK(C_PUNCTUATOR_COUNT <= 64);
   202: 
   203: // One shift and one mask answer what a chain of dependent compares answers.
   204: // Callers that ask several punctuator questions about the same token pay one
   205: // serial branch ladder per token otherwise; this is the classification they
   206: // gate on instead.  Inline for the same reason c_token_length is: an
   207: // out-of-line call in a per-token loop costs more than the body, and it
   208: // perturbs the caller's register allocation in every copy.
   209: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_punctuator_in_set(u8 punctuator, u64 set)
   210: {
   211:     return ((set >> punctuator) & 1) != 0;
   212: }

   277: // What one lex consumed, measured in the units a human uses to describe
   278: // source: bytes, lines, code, comments, whitespace. Every field falls out of
   279: // a branch c_lex_space already takes, so measuring costs a counter update per
   280: // whitespace byte, per comment and per line rather than a second pass.
   281: //
   282: // Two exact partitions hold per lex, and survive summation:
   283: //   translated_bytes == code_bytes + comment_bytes + blank_bytes
   284: //   translated_lines == code_lines + (comment_lines - mixed_lines) + blank_lines
   285: // `code_bytes` is derived, not stored: translated_bytes - comment - blank.
   286: //
   287: // `bytes`/`lines` measure the file as written; `translated_bytes`/
   288: // `translated_lines` measure what the lexer actually scanned, after
   289: // translation phases 1-2 delete carriage returns and fold line splices.
   290: // `spliced_lines` is the difference in lines, and the two byte counts differ
   291: // by that plus the deleted carriage returns.
   292: typedef struct CSourceMetrics CSourceMetrics;
   293: struct CSourceMetrics
   294: {
   295:     // Lexed aggregates count one per inclusion, unique aggregates one per
   296:     // distinct path; a single lex reports 1.
   297:     u64 files;
   298:     u64 bytes;
   299:     u64 translated_bytes;

   539: 
   540: // Location-recovery state of one preprocess run: the source map over the
   541: // spelling space, and the commit-on-demand arena owning that space. Behind
   542: // one pointer because CPreprocessResult travels by value through the whole
   543: // parse and lowering surface — growing that struct taxes every call.
   544: //
   545: // The map partitions the space into IrSourceRegions: TEXT regions recover
   546: // line/column through the owning lex result's checkpoints with the #line
   547: // delta of the region, and STAMP regions carry one position shared by every
   548: // offset in them (macro expansion output copies its spellings contiguously,
   549: // so one region covers one invocation's tokens). The preprocessor and every
   550: // IR consumer read it through the same `ir_source_map_position`, so a
   551: // token's position and the position an IR range resolves to are one lookup
   552: // over one structure — the program keeps a pointer to this very map.
   553: typedef struct CSourceMapRecovery CSourceMapRecovery;
   554: struct CSourceMapRecovery
   555: {
   556:     // Must outlive every consumer of the tokens; a caller compiling many
   557:     // units may destroy it once the unit's compilation is complete.
   558:     Arena* spelling_arena;
   559:     // Owns the result's token array itself: the preprocessor streams output
   560:     // tokens into a private arena so each lands in its final slot once.
   561:     // Same lifetime contract as the spelling arena — destroy both together.
   562:     Arena* token_arena;
   563:     // Owns the one-byte kind|punctuator projection beside the token rows.
   564:     // Keeping it in a separate private arena preserves the row stream's
   565:     // contiguous layout while giving parser shape walks a linear byte scan.
   566:     Arena* token_shape_arena;
   567:     CTokenShape* token_shapes;
   568:     IrSourceMap map;
   569:     // Region-array capacity, kept across the respell pass that may append.
   570:     u32 capacity;
   571:     u32 reserved;
   572: };

  1178: // Definition token -> lowest type id ever given that definition_start.
  1179: // c_parse_scalar_type_core_begin is the only writer of definition_start; a
  1180: // row that holds a start later is that row, a younger copy of it, or one of
  1181: // them restored by rollback, so no live row with the start is older than the
  1182: // recorded id. A scan for rows defined at a token may therefore begin at that
  1183: // id, and skip the table when the start was never assigned. Like the
  1184: // aggregate lookup below, the header outlives rollback, recorded ids only
  1185: // ever decrease, and at most half the slots are occupied; an exhausted arena
  1186: // sets `incomplete` and sends every query back to a whole-table scan.

 37244:                                                               u32* expression_end_out)
 37245: {
 37246:     if (start + 2 >= end || end > builder->preprocess.token_count || builder->preprocess.tokens[start].kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER ||

 37251:     }

 37254:     {
 37255:         return false;
 37256:     }

 37258:     u32 parentheses = 0;
 37259:     u32 brackets = 0;
 37260:     u32 braces = 0;

 37264:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 37265:         {
 37266:             parentheses += 1;
 37267:         }
 37268:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS) && parentheses)
 37269:         {
 37270:             parentheses -= 1;
 37271:         }
 37272:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))
 37273:         {
 37274:             brackets += 1;
 37275:         }
 37276:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET) && brackets)
 37277:         {
 37278:             brackets -= 1;
 37279:         }
 37280:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))

 37285:         {
 37286:             braces -= 1;
 37287:         }
 37288:         else if (!parentheses && !brackets && !braces && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
 37289:         {
 37290:             comma = index;
 37291:             break;
 37292:         }
 37293:     }
 37294:     *expression_start_out = start + 2;
 37295:     *expression_end_out = comma < close ? comma : close;

 37544: // Is this C type a pointer to a variably modified array -- `char (*p)[width]`,
 37545: // which musl's lsearch and lfind walk their tables with? Such a pointer never
 37546: // reaches c_type_ir_map, because the array it addresses has no constant size.
 37547: // It is the type `char p[][width]` adjusts to, so the local it becomes is the
 37548: // same one an array parameter becomes: a pointer to the innermost element,
 37549: // carrying the runtime dimension table that scales an index by the row size.
 37550: // The pointee is what the caller hands the layout walk, which prepends the
 37551: // absent outermost dimension.
 37552: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_ir_pointer_to_variable_array(CIntegerIrBuilder* builder, CTypeId type, CTypeId* array_out, IrTypeId* element_out)
 37553: {
 37554:     bool result = false;
 37555:     CType* pointer = c_type_from_id(&builder->parse, type);
 37556:     CTypeId array_type = pointer && pointer->kind == C_TYPE_POINTER ? pointer->element_type : C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 37557:     CType* array = c_type_from_id(&builder->parse, array_type);
 37558:     if (array && array->kind == C_TYPE_ARRAY && builder->c_type_ir_map[array_type.value].value == IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
 37559:     {
 37560:         CTypeId element_c_type = array_type;
 37561:         CType* element_c = array;
 37562:         while (element_c && element_c->kind == C_TYPE_ARRAY)
 37563:         {
 37564:             element_c_type = element_c->element_type;
 37565:             element_c = c_type_from_id(&builder->parse, element_c_type);
 37566:         }
 37567:         IrTypeId element_type = element_c ? builder->c_type_ir_map[element_c_type.value] : IR_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 37568:         IrType* element = ir_type_from_id(&builder->program->types, element_type);
 37569:         result = element && element->layout.resolved && element->layout.size;
 37570:         if (result)
 37571:         {
 37572:             *array_out = array_type;
 37573:             *element_out = element_type;
 37574:         }
 37575:     }
 37576: 
 37577:     return result;
 37578: }

 50358:     // misaligned for the type that reaches it. Eight bytes is the honest
 50359:     // product, and that is precisely why the declaration is refused instead of

 50361:     //
 50362:     // Only c_ir_add_aligned_type can produce a size that is not a multiple of
 50363:     // its alignment: a scalar's alignment divides its size by construction, an
 50364:     // aggregate rounds its size up to its own `aligned`, and a vector's size
 50365:     // is its alignment. Its one request comes from
 50366:     // CParseResult.type_alignments, so a translation unit that wrote no
 50367:     // aligned typedef -- almost every one -- skips the scan on a single
 50368:     // compare. The scan runs after the mapping rounds, over a settled table,
 50369:     // which is what makes one report per array automatic -- a type mapped in
 50370:     // an earlier round is not revisited -- and it reaches a typedef no object
 50371:     // ever names.

   530: // One well-known name as a set bit. The ids stay below 64 so any group of
   531: // them is a u64 the membership test shifts by the token's own symbol,
   532: // turning a ladder of spelling compares into one shift, one mask and one
   533: // branch.

     1: // Parsing and semantic analysis, the second stage of the frontend. Two
     2: // passes over the preprocessed token stream, entered through c_parse_ast and
     3: // c_analyze_semantics at the bottom of the file (c_parse runs both):
     4: // - c_parse_ast is one linear scan that splits the stream into top-level
     5: //   CParserDeclaration records â token extents, the name token, the body
     6: //   range, and typedef/constexpr/variadic flags â by delimiter counting
     7: //   alone. It builds no tree; every later consumer re-walks token ranges.
     8: //   The same top-level/body walks validate active integer token spellings,
     9: //   including unevaluated operands, before any type-only shortcut can hide one.
    10: //   A function body is split into statements the same way, and the one fact
    11: //   kept about them is the token range of each _Static_assert statement
    12: //   (CParserStaticAssert), which c_parse_bind_function_static_asserts
    13: //   checks against the block scopes the body binder creates. Body splitting
    14: //   reuses one depth-sized CParserBodyFrames vector; published declarations
    15: //   and assertion ranges remain owned by the translation-unit arena.
    16: //   One record is one declarator, not one declaration: a comma-separated
    17: //   list is split into a record per declarator, each keeping the shared
    18: //   specifiers in token_start/token_count and its own declarator segment in
    19: //   declarator_start/declarator_count.
    20: // - c_analyze_semantics sizes its tables from a token census
    21: //   (c_parse_token_census, vectorized), then builds the CParseResult the
    22: //   lowering stage consumes: interned types, entities, scopes, and
    23: //   diagnostics.
    24: //
    25: // Types and declarators are parsed by CTypeParseMachine (types in
    26: // c_internal.h), an explicit frame stack in place of recursion: each
    27: // CTypeParseFrameKind has a c_type_parse_*_step function dispatched by
    28: // c_type_parse_machine_run. Speculative parses record every published-type
    29: // mutation (c_type_parse_record_mutation) so c_type_parse_rollback can
    30: // restore the shared type table after a failed attempt.
    31: //
    32: // Layout, in file order; each anchor is a definition to search for:
    33: //   c_parse_token_class_compute,                  keyword and token
    34: //   c_parse_position_index_build                  classification, the
    35: //                                                 matching-delimiter index
    36: //   c_parse_builtin_type_layout,                  target-dependent type
    37: //   c_parse_type_layout                           sizes/alignments, aggregate
    38: //                                                 and bit-field layout
    39: //   c_semantic_check_named_call_arities          bound call constraints without IR
    40: //   c_parse_bfloat16_builtin,                    target builtin signatures
    41: //   c_parse_validate_bfloat16_builtin_calls       checked before unused pruning
    42: //   c_parse_direct_expression_base ..             expression typing without
    43: //   c_parse_conditional_expression_type           lowering (usual arithmetic
    44: //                                                 conversions, precedence,
    45: //                                                 and the null-pointer-
    46: //                                                 constant rule a conditional
    47: //                                                 picks a pointer type with)
    48: //   c_parse_constant_expression_evaluate          shared original-token-range
    49: //   c_parse_static_assert_evaluate                evaluator and _Static_assert
    50: //                                                 deferral past unresolved bounds
    51: //   c_parse_token_range_text,                     the one-line source quote a
    52: //   c_parse_static_assert_check                   non-constant assertion prints
    53: //   c_parse_initializer_designator,               initializer shapes and
    54: //   c_parse_infer_initializer_array_count_core    array-bound inference
    55: //   c_parse_add_type, c_parse_aggregate_lookup,   type interning and
    56: //   c_parse_primitive_type                        construction, attributes
    57: //   c_parse_definition_scan_start                 definition-token index
    58: //   c_parse_enum_complete, c_parse_enum_successor enum types and full-width values
    59: //   c_parse_word_bits_compute,                    specifier words answered
    60: //   c_parse_word_bits_token                       from the interned symbol
    61: //                                                 id (C_WORD_* bits), with
    62: //                                                 the spelling ladders as
    63: //                                                 the symbol-0 fallback
    64: //   c_type_parse_alignment_step ..                the type-parse machine
    65: //   c_type_parse_machine_run                      steps
    66: //   c_parse_scalar_type_core_begin,               declarators: pointers,
    67: //   c_parse_pointer_chain, c_parse_array_suffixes arrays, parameters
    68: //   c_parse_validate_constexpr_declaration,       constexpr, type
    69: //   c_parse_types_compatible                      compatibility
    70: //   c_parse_validate_cleanup_attribute            __attribute__((cleanup))
    71: //   c_parse_name_symbol .. c_parse_lookup_*       symbol interning, scopes,
    72: //                                                 entity lookup
    73: //   c_parse_local_declarations                    block-scope declarations,
    74: //                                                 auto inference, local
    75: //                                                 functions
    76: //   c_parse_label_address_prefix_proven,          statement boundaries, asm
    77: //   c_parse_statement_end                         goto, label addresses
    78: //   c_parse_bind_function_body                    binds body identifiers to
    79: //                                                 entities, indexes scopes
    80: //   c_parse_token_census_reference,               the token-shape census that
    81: //   c_parse_token_census                          sizes c_analyze_semantics'
    82: //                                                 tables: contiguous sidecar
    83: //                                                 shape compares plus a
    84: //                                                 symbol-byte projection for
    85: //                                                 the rare `for` spelling,
    86: //                                                 with the scalar reference
    87: //                                                 kept as the differential gate
    88: //   c_parse_typed_constant                       target-typed scalar constant evaluation
    89: //   c_parse_validate_lowering_constraints        source validation before canonical IR
    90: //   c_parse_ast, c_analyze_semantics, c_parse     model-building stage entry points
    91: //   c_analyze_semantics_only                     complete validation without canonical IR

  5066: // Bounds the controlling expression of one static assertion.  Buster applies
  5067: // the C integer-constant-expression constraint in every dialect; GNU modes do
  5068: // not adopt GCC's extension that accepts a floating scalar constant here.
  5069: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_static_assert_expression_range(CPreprocessResult preprocess, CDeclaration declaration,
  5070:                                                                 u32* expression_start_out, u32* expression_end_out)
  5071: {
  5072:     u32 start = declaration.token_start;
  5073:     u32 end = start + declaration.token_count;
  5074:     u32 comma = end;
  5075:     u32 close = end;
  5076:     u32 depth = 0;
  5077:     u32 group_depth = 0;
  5078:     bool valid = start + 3 < end && end <= preprocess.token_count &&
  5079:                  c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[start + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);

  5353: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_static_assert_evaluate(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParseResult* result,
  5354:                                                         CDeclaration declaration, CScopeId scope, u64* value_out, String8* message_out,
  5355:                                                         bool* requires_typed_evaluation_out)
  5356: {
  5357:     if (requires_typed_evaluation_out)
  5358:     {
  5359:         *requires_typed_evaluation_out = false;
  5360:     }
  5361:     u32 start = declaration.token_start;
  5362:     u32 end = start + declaration.token_count;
  5363:     bool valid = start + 3 < end && end <= preprocess.token_count &&
  5364:                  c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[start + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);
  5365:     u32 comma = end;
  5366:     u32 close = end;
  5367:     u32 depth = 0;
  5368:     // Braces and brackets carry commas of their own -- a compound literal
  5369:     // operand (`_Static_assert(sizeof (struct S){1, 2} == 8, "...")`) or a
  5370:     // subscript -- and neither can close the assert's parenthesis, so they get
  5371:     // their own depth. Counting only parentheses takes the literal's first
  5372:     // comma for the message separator and truncates the expression.
  5373:     u32 group_depth = 0;
  5374:     for (u32 token_index = start + 1; valid && token_index < end; token_index += 1)
  5375:     {
  5376:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[token_index];
  5377:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))
  5378:         {
  5379:             group_depth += 1;
  5380:         }
  5381:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET))
  5382:         {
  5383:             group_depth -= group_depth != 0;
  5384:         }
  5385:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
  5386:         {
  5387:             depth += 1;
  5388:         }
  5389:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS))
  5390:         {
  5391:             if (!depth)
  5392:             {
  5393:                 valid = false;
  5394:             }
  5395:             else if (!--depth)
  5396:             {
  5397:                 close = token_index;
  5398:                 break;
  5399:             }
  5400:         }
  5401:         else if (depth == 1 && !group_depth && comma == end && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
  5402:         {
  5403:             comma = token_index;
  5404:         }
  5405:     }
  5406:     u32 expression_end = comma < close ? comma : close;
  5407:     valid = valid && close != end && expression_end > start + 2;
  5408:     if (valid)
  5409:     {
  5410:         if (comma < close && comma + 1 < close && preprocess.tokens[comma + 1].kind == C_TOKEN_STRING_LITERAL)
  5411:         {
  5412:             *message_out = c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[comma + 1]);
  5413:         }
  5414:         valid = c_parse_constant_expression_evaluate(machine, arena, preprocess, result, scope, start + 2, expression_end,
  5415:                                                       value_out, requires_typed_evaluation_out);
  5416:     }
  5417:     return valid;
  5418: }

  5420: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_integer_constant_range(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParseResult* result,
  5421:                                                         CScopeId scope, u32 start, u32 end, u64* value_out)
  5422: {
  5423:     // Keep the original token indices: both the shape sidecar and the parse
  5424:     // position index describe this stream, not a copied assertion wrapper.
--- lines 5433-5594 ---
  5433: // C11 6.3.2.3p3 gives a null pointer constant two spellings: an integer
  5434: // constant expression with the value 0, or such an expression cast to
  5435: // `void *`.  The second is the only one that survives as a conditional
  5436: // operand, and it is what musl's <tgmath.h> selects a type with --
  5437: // `0 ? (t *)0 : (void *)!(c)` names `t` exactly when the `void *` arm is one.
  5438: //
  5439: // The type gate is not a shortcut, it is the whole correctness of the answer:
  5440: // the constant walk below strips every cast before evaluating, so
  5441: // `(double *)0` folds to zero just as `(void *)0` does, and without the gate
  5442: // any zero-valued pointer cast would claim to be a null pointer constant.
  5443: // The walk is entered machineless -- the caller is already inside a type-parse
  5444: // frame -- so an operand whose value needs the machine (a `sizeof` over a type
  5445: // only a frame can resolve) simply answers no, which is the conservative half.
  5446: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_range_is_null_pointer_constant(Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParseResult* result, CScopeId scope,
  5447:                                                                 CTypeId type_id, u32 start, u32 end)
  5448: {
  5449:     type_id = c_parse_unqualified_type(result, type_id);
  5450:     bool constant = false;
  5451:     if (type_id.value < result->type_count && start < end)
  5452:     {
  5453:         // By value: c_parse_unqualified_type may append a type and move the
  5454:         // table out from under a borrowed row.
  5455:         CType type = result->types[type_id.value];
  5456:         bool void_pointer = false;
  5457:         if (type.kind == C_TYPE_POINTER)
  5458:         {
  5459:             CTypeId element = c_parse_unqualified_type(result, type.element_type);
  5460:             void_pointer = element.value < result->type_count && result->types[element.value].kind == C_TYPE_VOID;
  5461:         }
  5462:         if (void_pointer || c_parse_expression_integer_kind(type.kind))
  5463:         {
  5464:             // Strip the pointer casts in front of the constant by shape, not
  5465:             // by resolving them: a parenthesized group whose last token is
  5466:             // `*` is never a parenthesized expression, so it can only be a
  5467:             // pointer cast.  The walk below resolves a cast type of its own,
  5468:             // but only through the machineless base-type reader, which cannot
  5469:             // read the `(__typeof__(...) *)` musl's `__type2` writes around
  5470:             // its inner `__type1` -- and one unstripped cast is the whole
  5471:             // answer, because the operand it hides is the literal `0`.
  5472:             while (start < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[start], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
  5473:             {
  5474:                 u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter(preprocess, start, end, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS);
  5475:                 if (close + 1 >= end || close < start + 2 || !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[close - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_STAR))
  5476:                 {
  5477:                     break;
  5478:                 }
  5479:                 start = close + 1;
  5480:             }
  5481:             u64 value = 0;
  5482:             constant = c_parse_integer_constant_range(0, arena, preprocess, result, scope, start, end, &value) && !value;
  5483:         }
  5484:     }
  5485:     return constant;
  5486: }

  5488: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_static_assert_has_unresolved_array(CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParseResult* result, CDeclaration declaration, CScopeId scope)
  5489: {
  5490:     u32 start = declaration.token_start + 2;
  5491:     u32 end = declaration.token_start + declaration.token_count;
  5492:     for (u32 token_index = start; token_index < end; token_index += 1)
  5493:     {
  5494:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[token_index];
  5495:         if (token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)
  5496:         {
  5497:             continue;
  5498:         }
  5499:         CEntityId entity_id = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, scope, &token);
  5500:         if (entity_id.value >= result->entity_count)
  5501:         {
  5502:             continue;
  5503:         }
  5504:         CEntity* entity = result->entities + entity_id.value;
  5505:         if (entity->kind != C_ENTITY_LOCAL && entity->kind != C_ENTITY_OBJECT)
  5506:         {
  5507:             continue;
  5508:         }
  5509:         CTypeId type_id = entity->type;
  5510:         while (type_id.value < result->type_count && result->types[type_id.value].has_unqualified_type)
  5511:         {
  5512:             type_id = result->types[type_id.value].unqualified_type;
  5513:         }
  5514:         if (type_id.value < result->type_count)
  5515:         {
  5516:             CType* type = result->types + type_id.value;
  5517:             if (type->kind == C_TYPE_ARRAY && type->array_bound < result->array_bound_count &&
  5518:                 !result->array_bounds[type->array_bound].token_count && !result->array_bounds[type->array_bound].is_star &&
  5519:                 !result->array_bounds[type->array_bound].has_inferred_count)
  5520:             {
  5521:                 return true;
  5522:             }
  5523:         }
  5524:     }
  5525:     return false;
  5526: }

  5528: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_defer_static_assert(CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParseResult* result, CDeclaration declaration, CScopeId scope)
  5529: {
  5530:     if (!result || result->deferred_static_assert_count >= result->deferred_static_assert_capacity)
  5531:     {
  5532:         return;
  5533:     }
  5534:     CSourceLocation location = declaration.location;
  5535:     if (declaration.token_start < preprocess.token_count)
  5536:     {
  5537:         location = c_preprocess_token_location(&preprocess, preprocess.tokens[declaration.token_start]);
  5538:     }
  5539:     result->deferred_static_asserts[result->deferred_static_assert_count++] = (CDeferredStaticAssert){
  5540:         .token_start = declaration.token_start,
  5541:         .token_count = declaration.token_count,
  5542:         .scope = scope,
  5543:         .location = location,
  5544:     };
  5545: }
  5546: 
  5547: // The tokens [start, end) as a diagnostic quotes them: on one line, touching
  5548: // where they touched in the source and otherwise one space apart, so a line
  5549: // break, a comment or a macro boundary reads as the space it stood for. This
  5550: // is the -E printer's adjacency rule (compiler_driver_preprocess_text) with
  5551: // line breaks folded, guarded by the same c_token_requires_separator.
  5552: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL String8 c_parse_token_range_text(Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end)
  5553: {
  5554:     u64 capacity = 1;
  5555:     for (u32 index = start; index < end; index += 1)
  5556:     {
  5557:         capacity += c_token_length(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[index]) + 1;
  5558:     }
  5559:     char8* text = arena_allocate(arena, char8, capacity);
  5560:     u64 length = 0;
  5561:     CSourceLocation previous_location = {0};
  5562:     CToken previous_token = {0};
  5563:     String8 previous_spelling = {0};
  5564:     for (u32 index = start; index < end; index += 1)
  5565:     {
  5566:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
  5567:         CSourceLocation location = c_preprocess_token_location(&preprocess, token);
  5568:         String8 spelling = c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token);
  5569:         if (index != start &&
  5570:             (location.file != previous_location.file || location.line != previous_location.line ||
  5571:              location.column != previous_location.column + previous_spelling.length ||
  5572:              c_token_requires_separator(previous_token, previous_spelling, token, spelling)))
  5573:         {
  5574:             text[length++] = ' ';
  5575:         }
  5576:         memcpy(text + length, spelling.pointer, spelling.length);
  5577:         length += spelling.length;
  5578:         previous_location = location;
  5579:         previous_token = token;
  5580:         previous_spelling = spelling;
  5581:     }
  5582:     text[length] = 0;
  5583:     return (String8){
  5584:         .pointer = text,

  5891: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_arena_can_allocate(Arena* arena, u64 size, u64 alignment)
  5892: {
  5893:     if (!arena || !alignment || !arena->granularity || alignment - 1 > UINT64_MAX - arena->position)
  5894:     {
  5895:         return false;
  5896:     }
  5897:     u64 position = (arena->position + alignment - 1) & ~(alignment - 1);
  5898:     if (position > arena->reserved_size || size > arena->reserved_size - position)
  5899:     {
  5900:         return false;
  5901:     }
  5902:     u64 end = position + size;
  5903:     u64 granularity = arena->granularity;
  5904:     if (granularity - 1 > UINT64_MAX - end)
  5905:     {
  5906:         return false;
  5907:     }
  5908:     u64 committed_end = (end + granularity - 1) & ~(granularity - 1);
  5909:     return committed_end <= arena->reserved_size;
  5910: }

  5912: BUSTER_C_SHARED bool c_parse_result_reserve_types(CParseResult* result, u32 additional)
  5913: {
  5914:     if (!result || !additional || additional <= result->type_capacity - BUSTER_MIN(result->type_count, result->type_capacity))
  5915:     {
  5916:         return result != 0;
  5917:     }
  5918:     u64 required = (u64)result->type_count + additional;
  5919:     if (required > UINT32_MAX || !result->arena)
  5920:     {
  5921:         return false;
  5922:     }
  5923:     u32 capacity = result->type_capacity ? result->type_capacity : 1;
  5924:     while ((u64)capacity < required)
  5925:     {
  5926:         if (capacity > UINT32_MAX / 2)
  5927:         {
  5928:             capacity = (u32)required;
  5929:             break;
  5930:         }
  5931:         capacity *= 2;
  5932:     }

 10130: // The first token past a parenthesized declarator starting at the `(` at
 10131: // `open`: its group, then any run of parameter-list and array groups with
 10132: // attribute lists between them. The parenthesized frame requires its range to
 10133: // end exactly there and fails without a word when it does not, so the
 10134: // segment asks this only after that failure, to tell trailing tokens from a
 10135: // malformed group; the declarators that parse never pay for the scan.
 10136: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL u32 c_parse_parenthesized_declarator_extent(CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 open, u32 end)
 10137: {
 10138:     u32 index = open;
 10139:     u32 previous = end;
 10140:     while (index < end && index != previous)
 10141:     {
 10142:         previous = index;
 10143:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 10144:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))
 10145:         {
 10146:             bool parenthesis = c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);
 10147:             u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter(preprocess, index, end, parenthesis ? C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS : C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET,
 10148:                                                    parenthesis ? C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS : C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET);
 10149:             index = close < end ? close + 1 : index;
 10150:         }
 10151:         index = c_parse_skip_attributes(preprocess, index, end);
 10152:     }
 10153:     return index;
 10154: }

 10156: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_type_parse_aggregate_segment_step(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CTypeParseFrame* frame)
 10157: {
 10158:     CParseResult* result = frame->result;

 10161:     {

 10177:                 {

 10179:                 }

 10181:                 {
 10182:                     depth -= 1;
 10183:                     if (!depth)
 10184:                     {
 10185:                         assertion_end = index + 1;
 10186:                     }
 10187:                 }
 10188:             }

 10190:             bool deferred = false;

 10192:             {

 17897: BUSTER_C_SHARED void c_parse_bind_function_body(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* result_arena, CParseResult* result,
 17898:                                                     CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 declaration_index)
 17899: {
 17900:     CDeclaration* declaration = &result->declarations[declaration_index];
 17901:     if (declaration->is_definition && declaration->body_token_count)
 17902:     {

 17904:                                       declaration->body_token_count);
 17905:         // The body's scopes are closed and their bindings unwound, but the
 17906:         // bucket chains keep every entity they ever held, so the two passes
 17907:         // below -- which re-enter a scope through c_parse_scope_for_token,
 17908:         // the function scope included -- must ask the chains.  Retiring the
 17909:         // array here is what makes the two answers agree.
 17910:         result->binding_scope = C_SCOPE_ID_INVALID;

 17912:         // After the walk above, because it needs the block scopes that walk creates.

 17914:                                            declaration->body_start + declaration->body_token_count);

 17918: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_type_only_declaration(CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end)
 17919: {
 17920:     // GNU attributes may sit before the aggregate keyword, between it and the
 17921:     // tag (`struct __attribute__((__may_alias__)) sockaddr_storage { ... };`,
 17922:     // which is how glibc declares it), and after the tag. Skipping them keeps
 17923:     // such a definition classified as a type-only declaration; read as an
 17924:     // object declaration instead, the attribute's name becomes the declared
 17925:     // object and the aggregate is never defined.

 17927:     while (index < end && preprocess.tokens[index].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER &&
 17928:            c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[index], C_PARSE_TYPE_ONLY_PREFIX_KEYWORDS))
 17929:     {
 17930:         index = c_parse_skip_attributes(preprocess, index + 1, end);
 17931:     }
 17932:     if (index >= end || preprocess.tokens[index].kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER ||
 17933:         !c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[index], C_PARSE_AGGREGATE_KEYWORDS))
 17934:     {
 17935:         return false;
 17936:     }
 17937:     index = c_parse_skip_attributes(preprocess, index + 1, end);
 17938:     if (index < end && preprocess.tokens[index].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)
 17939:     {
 17940:         index += 1;
 17941:     }
 17942:     index = c_parse_skip_attributes(preprocess, index, end);
 17943:     if (index < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index], C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON))
 17944:     {
 17945:         while (index < end && !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 17946:         {
 17947:             index += 1;
 17948:         }
 17949:     }
 17950:     if (index < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 17951:     {

 17953:         index += 1;

 17955:         {

 17957:             {
 17958:                 depth += 1;
 17959:             }
 17960:             else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index], C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE))
 17961:             {
 17962:                 depth -= 1;
 17963:             }
 17964:             index += 1;
 17965:         }

--- lines 18127-18248 ---
 18127: //
 18128: // The `(` is what makes the shape unambiguous. A declaration's specifiers
 18129: // cannot be preceded by one, so nothing a declaration owns is registered twice
 18130: // here, and the check that no type already carries this definition keeps a
 18131: // range that is somehow reached twice from defining two types.
 18132: //
 18133: // The range is a token range rather than a function body because a file-scope
 18134: // initializer is written the same way and is not walked by anything else:
 18135: // musl's `ioctl.c` sizes its `compat_map` entries with
 18136: // `sizeof(struct { int i; time_t t; char c[64]; })`, an anonymous aggregate
 18137: // defined inside the initializer of a file-scope object. Left unregistered,
 18138: // the sizeof fold has no type to resolve and the whole initializer is refused.
 18139: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_bind_expression_aggregates(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 18140:                                                             CScopeId root_scope, u32 start, u32 end)
 18141: {
 18142:     u32 body_end = end;
 18143:     if (body_end > preprocess.token_count)
 18144:     {
 18145:         body_end = (u32)preprocess.token_count;
 18146:     }
 18147:     for (u32 index = start; index + 2 < body_end; index += 1)
 18148:     {
 18149:         if (!c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 18150:         {
 18151:             continue;
 18152:         }
 18153:         u32 keyword = index + 1;
 18154:         u32 close = 0;
 18155:         if (!c_parse_aggregate_definition_at(preprocess, keyword, body_end, &close))
 18156:         {
 18157:             continue;
 18158:         }
 18159:         u32 open = keyword + 1;
 18160:         if (preprocess.tokens[open].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)
 18161:         {
 18162:             open += 1;
 18163:         }
 18164:         bool defined = false;
 18165:         for (u32 type_index = c_parse_definition_scan_start(result, open + 1); type_index < result->type_count && !defined; type_index += 1)
 18166:         {
 18167:             C_DEFINITION_INDEX_COUNT(result->definition_index, scan_row_count, 1);
 18168:             defined = result->types[type_index].definition_start == open + 1;
 18169:         }
 18170:         if (defined)
 18171:         {
 18172:             index = close;
 18173:             continue;
 18174:         }
 18175:         u32 declarator_start = 0;
 18176:         CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, root_scope, keyword);
 18177:         c_parse_scalar_type_in_scope(machine, result, preprocess, scope, keyword, close + 1, &declarator_start);
 18178:         index = close;
 18179:     }
 18180: }

 18182: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_bind_function_static_asserts(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* result_arena, CParseResult* result,
 18183:                                                               CPreprocessResult preprocess, CDeclaration* declaration)
 18184: {
 18185:     // The syntax pass noted the body's _Static_assert statements, at any
 18186:     // depth and in body order, as it split the body, so the body that has
 18187:     // none -- the common case by a wide margin -- costs one pointer test
 18188:     // here and nothing is walked to find the rest.
 18189:     CParserStaticAssert* assertion = declaration->syntax_declaration ? declaration->syntax_declaration->first_static_assert : 0;
 18190:     for (; assertion; assertion = assertion->next)
 18191:     {
 18192:         CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, assertion->token_start);
 18193:         c_parse_static_assert_check(machine, result_arena, preprocess, result,
 18194:                                     (CDeclaration){
 18195:                                         .token_start = assertion->token_start,
 18196:                                         .token_count = assertion->token_count,
 18197:                                     },
 18198:                                     scope);
 18199:     }
 18200: }

 18202: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parser_diagnostic(Arena* arena, CParserResult* result, CSourceLocation location, CDiagnosticKind kind, String8 message)
 18203: {
 18204:     if (result && result->diagnostic_count < result->diagnostic_capacity)
 18205:     {
 18206:         // Clean syntax needs no diagnostic storage. Allocate the original
 18207:         // bounded contiguous array once, before publishing its first row.
 18208:         if (!result->diagnostics)
 18209:         {
 18210:             result->diagnostics = arena_allocate(arena, CDiagnostic, result->diagnostic_capacity);
 18211:         }
 18212:         result->diagnostics[result->diagnostic_count++] = (CDiagnostic){
 18213:             .message = message,
 18214:             .location = location,
 18215:             .kind = kind,
 18216:         };
 18217:     }
 18218: }

 18287: typedef struct CParserBlockFrame CParserBlockFrame;
 18288: struct CParserBlockFrame
 18289: {
 18290:     u32 statement_start;
 18291:     u32 parenthesis_depth;
 18292:     u32 bracket_depth;
 18293: };
 18294: 
 18295: // One private, depth-sized vector per syntax pass, reused across bodies.
 18296: // No declaration or assertion retains a frame address. Growth copies only
 18297: // the live prefix; callers reacquire frames after a reserve.
 18298: #define C_PARSER_BODY_FRAME_INITIAL_CAPACITY 16u
 18299: 
 18300: typedef struct CParserBodyFrames CParserBodyFrames;
 18301: struct CParserBodyFrames
 18302: {
 18303:     CParserBlockFrame* data;
 18304:     u32 capacity;
 18305: };
 18306: 
 18307: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parser_body_frames_reserve(Arena* arena, CParserBodyFrames* frames, u32 count)
 18308: {
 18309:     if (count == frames->capacity)
 18310:     {
 18311:         // c_parse_ast caps tokens below 2^31. A live frame requires an
 18312:         // opening brace, so the last possible doubling is 2^30 -> 2^31.
 18313:         u32 capacity = frames->capacity ? frames->capacity * 2 : C_PARSER_BODY_FRAME_INITIAL_CAPACITY;
 18314:         CParserBlockFrame* data = arena_allocate(arena, CParserBlockFrame, capacity);
 18315:         if (count)
 18316:         {
 18317:             memcpy(data, frames->data, sizeof(*data) * count);
 18318:         }
 18319:         frames->data = data;
 18320:         frames->capacity = capacity;
 18321:     }
 18322: }
 18323: 
 18324: // Notes the statement [start, end) on the declaration if it is a
 18325: // _Static_assert.  A C23 attribute sequence may precede the keyword and
 18326: // stays inside the noted range, which begins where the statement does.  An
 18327: // empty or out-of-range statement is no statement and is dropped, as the
 18328: // statement-tree builder this replaces dropped it.
 18329: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parser_static_assert_note(Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParserDeclaration* declaration, u32 start, u32 end)
 18330: {
 18331:     if (arena && start < end && end <= preprocess.token_count)
 18332:     {
 18333:         u32 first = start;
 18334:         for (u32 attribute_end = 0; c_parse_c23_attribute_at(preprocess, first, end, &attribute_end);)
 18335:         {
 18336:             first = attribute_end;
 18337:         }
 18338:         if (first < end && preprocess.tokens[first].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER &&
 18339:             c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[first], C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_STATIC_ASSERT))
 18340:         {
 18341:             CParserStaticAssert* assertion = arena_allocate(arena, CParserStaticAssert, 1);
 18342:             *assertion = (CParserStaticAssert){
 18343:                 .token_start = start,
 18344:                 .token_count = end - start,
 18345:             };
 18346:             if (declaration->last_static_assert)
 18347:             {
 18348:                 declaration->last_static_assert->next = assertion;
 18349:             }
 18350:             else
 18351:             {
 18352:                 declaration->first_static_assert = assertion;
 18353:             }
 18354:             declaration->last_static_assert = assertion;
 18355:         }
 18356:     }
 18357: }
 18358: 
 18359: // Splits a function body into statements by delimiter counting -- a `;` at
 18360: // parenthesis and bracket depth zero ends one, `{` and `}` open and close a
 18361: // nested block whose statements are split the same way -- and notes each
 18362: // _Static_assert statement it meets, at any depth, in body order.  Nothing
 18363: // else about a statement is kept: the semantic pass rebinds the body from
 18364: // its tokens.  A `{` seen inside parentheses or brackets (a compound literal
 18365: // or a statement expression in an argument) belongs to the statement that
 18366: // spans it and opens no block.
 18367: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parser_parse_function_body(Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParserDeclaration* declaration,
 18368:                                                        CParserBodyFrames* body_frames)
 18369: {
 18370:     u32 body_start = declaration->body_start;
 18371:     u32 body_end = body_start + declaration->body_token_count;
 18372:     c_parser_body_frames_reserve(arena, body_frames, 0);
 18373:     CParserBlockFrame* frames = body_frames->data;
 18374:     u32 frame_count = 1;
 18375:     frames[0] = (CParserBlockFrame){
 18376:         .statement_start = body_start,
 18377:     };
 18378:     u32 index = body_start;
 18379:     while (index < body_end)
 18380:     {
 18381:         CParserBlockFrame* frame = &frames[frame_count - 1];
 18382:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 18383:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 18384:         {
 18385:             frame->parenthesis_depth += 1;
 18386:             index += 1;
 18387:             continue;
 18388:         }
 18389:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS))
 18390:         {
 18391:             frame->parenthesis_depth -= frame->parenthesis_depth != 0;
 18392:             index += 1;
 18393:             continue;
 18394:         }
 18395:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))
 18396:         {
 18397:             frame->bracket_depth += 1;
 18398:             index += 1;
 18399:             continue;
 18400:         }
 18401:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET))
 18402:         {
 18403:             frame->bracket_depth -= frame->bracket_depth != 0;
 18404:             index += 1;
 18405:             continue;
 18406:         }
 18407:         if (!frame->parenthesis_depth && !frame->bracket_depth && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 18408:         {
 18409:             BUSTER_VALIDATE(frame_count < declaration->body_token_count + 1);
 18410:             if (frame_count == body_frames->capacity)
 18411:             {
 18412:                 c_parser_body_frames_reserve(arena, body_frames, frame_count);
 18413:                 frames = body_frames->data;
 18414:             }
 18415:             frames[frame_count++] = (CParserBlockFrame){
 18416:                 .statement_start = index + 1,
 18417:             };
 18418:             index += 1;
 18419:             continue;
 18420:         }
 18421:         if (!frame->parenthesis_depth && !frame->bracket_depth && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE))
 18422:         {
 18423:             if (frame_count == 1)
 18424:             {
 18425:                 index += 1;
 18426:                 continue;
 18427:             }
 18428:             if (frame->statement_start < index)
 18429:             {
 18430:                 c_parser_static_assert_note(arena, preprocess, declaration, frame->statement_start, index);
 18431:             }
 18432:             frame_count -= 1;
 18433:             frames[frame_count - 1].statement_start = index + 1;
 18434:             index += 1;
 18435:             continue;
 18436:         }
 18437:         if (!frame->parenthesis_depth && !frame->bracket_depth && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON))
 18438:         {
 18439:             if (frame->statement_start < index + 1)
 18440:             {
 18441:                 c_parser_static_assert_note(arena, preprocess, declaration, frame->statement_start, index + 1);
 18442:             }
 18443:             frame->statement_start = index + 1;
 18444:         }
 18445:         index += 1;
 18446:     }
 18447:     // An unclosed block reaches the body's end with its last statement
 18448:     // pending, and so does every block enclosing it; each is noted from where
 18449:     // its own last statement began, innermost first.
 18450:     while (frame_count > 1)
 18451:     {
 18452:         CParserBlockFrame* frame = &frames[frame_count - 1];
 18453:         if (frame->statement_start < body_end)
 18454:         {
 18455:             c_parser_static_assert_note(arena, preprocess, declaration, frame->statement_start, body_end);
 18456:         }
 18457:         frame_count -= 1;
 18458:     }
 18459:     if (frames[0].statement_start < body_end)
 18460:     {
 18461:         c_parser_static_assert_note(arena, preprocess, declaration, frames[0].statement_start, body_end);
 18462:     }
 18463: }
 18464: 
 18465: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parser_parse_declaration_expression(CPreprocessResult preprocess, CParserDeclaration* declaration)
 18466: {
 18467:     // A declarator split out of a list owns only its own initializer; an
 18468:     // unsplit declaration owns its whole range.
 18469:     u32 start = declaration->declarator_count ? declaration->declarator_start : declaration->token_start;

 22762: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_alias_targets(Arena* arena, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess)
 22763: {
 22764:     for (u32 index = 0; index < result->declaration_count; index += 1)
 22765:     {
 22766:         CDeclaration declaration = result->declarations[index];
 22767:         if (declaration.entity.value < result->entity_count &&
 22768:             (declaration.kind == C_DECLARATION_OBJECT || declaration.kind == C_DECLARATION_FUNCTION))
 22769:         {
 22770:             CDeclarationBinding binding = c_declaration_binding(arena, preprocess, declaration);
 22771:             if (binding.alias_target.length)
 22772:             {
 22773:                 CEntityId target = result->scope_count ? c_parse_lookup_entity(result, (CScopeId){.value = 0}, binding.alias_target)
 22774:                                                       : C_ENTITY_ID_INVALID;
 22775:                 String8 message = {0};
 22776:                 if (target.value >= result->entity_count || target.value == declaration.entity.value)
 22777:                 {
 22778:                     message = string_format(arena, S8("alias target '{S8}' is not declared in this translation unit"), binding.alias_target);
 22779:                 }
 22780:                 else if (result->entities[declaration.entity.value].is_definition)
 22781:                 {
 22782:                     message = string_format(arena, S8("'{S8}' is both defined here and aliased to '{S8}'"), declaration.name, binding.alias_target);
 22783:                 }
 22784:                 else if (!result->entities[target.value].is_definition)
 22785:                 {
 22786:                     message = string_format(arena, S8("alias '{S8}' names '{S8}', which is not defined in this translation unit"),
 22787:                                             declaration.name, binding.alias_target);
 22788:                 }
 22789:                 if (message.length)
 22790:                 {
 22791:                     c_parse_diagnostic(result, declaration.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_UNSUPPORTED_SEMANTICS, message);
 22792:                 }
 22793:             }
 22794:         }
 22795:     }
 22796: }

 22798: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_deferred_assertions(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CParseResult* result,
 22799:                                                                CPreprocessResult preprocess)
 22800: {
 22801:     for (u32 index = 0; index < result->deferred_static_assert_count; index += 1)
 22802:     {
 22803:         CDeferredStaticAssert assertion = result->deferred_static_asserts[index];
 22804:         // A member assertion is deferred from its aggregate's scope; the
 22805:         // innermost block enclosing it is where its names bind.
 22806:         assertion.scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, assertion.scope, assertion.token_start);
 22807:         CDeclaration declaration = {.token_start = assertion.token_start, .token_count = assertion.token_count};
 22808:         u32 start = 0;
 22809:         u32 end = 0;
 22810:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22811:         CParseConstant value = {.type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID};
 22812:         if (c_parse_static_assert_expression_range(preprocess, declaration, &start, &end))
 22813:         {
 22814:             value = c_parse_typed_constant(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, assertion.scope, start, end);
 22815:         }
 22816:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 22817:         if (!value.valid)
 22818:         {
 22819:             c_parse_diagnostic(result, assertion.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_STATIC_ASSERT_NOT_CONSTANT,
 22820:                                S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression"));
 22821:         }
 22822:         else if (value.is_float || !c_parse_constant_truth(value))
 22823:         {
 22824:             c_parse_diagnostic(result, assertion.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_STATIC_ASSERT_FAILED,
 22825:                                S8("static assertion expression is not a true integer constant expression"));
 22826:         }
 22827:     }
 22828:     BUSTER_UNUSED(arena);
 22829: }

 22831: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_bit_field_widths(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CParseResult* result,
 22832:                                                           CPreprocessResult preprocess)
 22833: {
 22834:     for (u32 index = 0; index < result->member_count; index += 1)
 22835:     {
 22836:         CMember member = result->members[index];
 22837:         if (member.type.value < result->type_count && result->types[member.type.value].kind == C_TYPE_VOID)
 22838:         {
 22839:             c_parse_diagnostic(result, member.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_VOID_OBJECT, S8("a member may not have type 'void'"));
 22840:         }
 22841:         u32 alignment_token = member.alignment_start < result->alignment_count ? result->alignments[member.alignment_start].token_start : 0;
 22842:         CScopeId member_scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, (CScopeId){.value = 0}, alignment_token);
 22843:         String8 alignment_message = c_parse_validate_alignment_range(machine, result, preprocess, member_scope, member.type,
 22844:                                                                       member.alignment_start, member.alignment_count, 0);
 22845:         if (alignment_message.length)
 22846:         {
 22847:             c_parse_diagnostic(result, member.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_ALIGNMENT, alignment_message);
 22848:         }
 22849:         if (member.is_bit_field)
 22850:         {
 22851:             u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22852:             CParseConstant width = {.integer = member.bit_width, .valid = true};
 22853:             if (member.bit_width_token_count)
 22854:             {
 22855:                 CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, (CScopeId){.value = 0}, member.bit_width_token_start);
 22856:                 width = c_parse_typed_constant(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope,
 22857:                                                member.bit_width_token_start, member.bit_width_token_start + member.bit_width_token_count);
 22858:             }
 22859:             if (width.valid && width.is_float)
 22860:             {
 22861:                 c_parse_diagnostic(result, member.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_BIT_FIELD_WIDTH,
 22862:                                    S8("bit-field width is not an integer constant expression"));
 22863:             }
 22864:             else if (width.valid && !width.integer && member.name.length)
 22865:             {
 22866:                 c_parse_diagnostic(result, member.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_BIT_FIELD_WIDTH,
 22867:                                    string_format(arena, S8("named bit-field '{S8}' has zero width"), member.name));
 22868:             }
 22869:             arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 22870:         }
 22871:     }
 22872: }

  8030: BUSTER_C_SHARED bool c_preprocess_dialect_is_c23(CPreprocessDialect dialect)
  8031: {
  8032:     return dialect == C_PREPROCESS_DIALECT_GNU23 || dialect == C_PREPROCESS_DIALECT_C23;
  8033: }
  8034: 
  8035: // C23 spells the underscore keywords through aliases; the respell runs at
  8036: // the end of preprocessing while the spelling space is still open, so each
  8037: // respelled token points at prelude bytes copied under an expansion map
  8038: // entry that preserves its recovered source location.
  8039: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_preprocess_respell_c23(CSpellingSpace* space, CSourceMap* map, CPreprocessResult* result)
  8040: {
  8041:     if (!c_preprocess_dialect_is_c23(result->dialect))
  8042:     {
  8043:         return;
  8044:     }
  8045:     char8 const* base = space->base;
  8046:     IrSourceMapCursor cursor = IR_SOURCE_MAP_CURSOR_EMPTY;
  8047:     for (u64 index = 0; index < result->token_count; index += 1)
  8048:     {
  8049:         CToken* token = result->tokens + index;
  8050:         if (token->kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)
  8051:         {
  8052:             continue;
  8053:         }
  8054:         String8 spelling = c_token_spelling(base, *token);
  8055:         String8 respelled = {0};
  8056:         if (string_equal(spelling, S8("bool")))
  8057:         {
  8058:             respelled = S8("_Bool");
  8059:         }
  8060:         else if (string_equal(spelling, S8("alignas")))
  8061:         {
  8062:             respelled = S8("_Alignas");
  8063:         }
  8064:         else if (string_equal(spelling, S8("alignof")))
  8065:         {
  8066:             respelled = S8("_Alignof");
  8067:         }
  8068:         else if (string_equal(spelling, S8("static_assert")))
  8069:         {
  8070:             respelled = S8("_Static_assert");
  8071:         }
  8072:         else if (string_equal(spelling, S8("thread_local")))
  8073:         {
  8074:             respelled = S8("_Thread_local");
  8075:         }
  8076:         if (respelled.length)
  8077:         {
  8078:             CSourceLocation location = c_preprocess_token_location_cursor(result, *token, &cursor);
  8079:             CToken copy = c_space_token(space, respelled, C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER, C_PUNCTUATOR_NONE);
  8080:             c_source_map_append(map, (IrSourceRegion){
  8081:                                          .start = copy.offset,
  8082:                                          .source = location.file,
  8083:                                          .stamp = c_position_from_source_location(location),
  8084:                                          .kind = IR_SOURCE_REGION_STAMP,
  8085:                                          .origin_plus_one = location.map_offset + 1,
  8086:                                      });
  8087:             // Appends can move the region array; keep the result's view (the
  8088:             // recovery above reads through it) current. The new region is at
  8089:             // the tail, past every key already published, so the lookups keep
  8090:             // answering through the keys until the map is republished.
  8091:             result->recovery->map.regions = map->regions;
  8092:             token->offset = copy.offset;
  8093:             token->length = copy.length;
  8094:             // The symbol travels with the spelling: a respelled token must
  8095:             // re-intern or every symbol-keyed consumer would classify it as
  8096:             // the old name.
  8097:             token->symbol = result->symbols ? c_symbol_intern(result->symbols, respelled) : 0;
  8098:         }
  8099:     }
  8100: }
  8101:

   196: // Static assertions containing local-object sizeof operands must agree in the
   197: // semantic-only and object actions, including both frontend SSA forms.
   198: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult compiler_driver_test_local_sizeof_static_asserts(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
   199: {
   200:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
   201:     struct
   202:     {
   203:         String8 source;
   204:         bool valid;
   205:         String8 diagnostic_fragment;
   206:     } cases[] = {
   207:         {S8("typedef long T;\n"
   208:             "int f(void) {\n"
   209:             "    T a = 0;\n"
   210:             "    {\n"
   211:             "        typedef char T;\n"
   212:             "        T b = 0;\n"
   213:             "        _Static_assert(sizeof b == 1, \"inner\");\n"
   214:             "    }\n"
   215:             "    return sizeof a;\n"
   216:             "}\n"), true, {0}},
   217:         {S8("int f(void) {\n"
   218:             "    int a[4];\n"
   219:             "    _Static_assert(sizeof a == 4 * sizeof(int), \"array\");\n"
   220:             "    return _Generic(a, int *: 1);\n"
   221:             "}\n"), true, {0}},
   222:         {S8("int f(void) {\n"
   223:             "    char value;\n"
   224:             "    _Static_assert(sizeof (value) == 1, \"parenthesized scalar\");\n"
   225:             "    return sizeof value;\n"
   226:             "}\n"), true, {0}},
   227:         {S8("int f(void) {\n"
   228:             "    int values[4];\n"
   229:             "    _Static_assert(sizeof (values) == 4 * sizeof(int), \"parenthesized array\");\n"
   230:             "    return sizeof values;\n"
   231:             "}\n"), true, {0}},
   232:         {S8("int f(int n) {\n"
   233:             "    _Static_assert(sizeof n == n, \"runtime value\");\n"
   234:             "    return n;\n"
   235:             "}\n"), false, S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression")},
   236:         {S8("int f(int n) {\n"
   237:             "    int values[n];\n"
   238:             "    _Static_assert(sizeof values == n * sizeof(int), \"variable array\");\n"
   239:             "    return (int)sizeof values;\n"
   240:             "}\n"), false, S8("static assertion expression is not an integer constant expression")},
   241:         {S8("int f(void) {\n"
   242:             "    char value;\n"
   243:             "    _Static_assert(sizeof value == 2, \"false\");\n"
   244:             "    return 0;\n"
   245:             "}\n"), false, S8("static assertion expression is not a true integer constant expression")},
   246:     };
   247:     String8 forms[] = {S8("-ffrontend-ssa"), S8("-fno-frontend-ssa")};
   248:     for (u32 case_index = 0; case_index < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cases); case_index += 1)
   249:     {
   250:         TemporalArena temporary = arena_begin_temporal(arguments->arena);
   251:         Arena* arena = temporary.arena;
   252:         String8 input = buster_test_temporary_path(arena, S8("buster-local-sizeof-static-assert"), S8(".c"));
   253:         BUSTER_TEST(arguments, file_write(input, BUSTER_SLICE_TO_BYTE_SLICE(cases[case_index].source)));
   254:         for (u32 form = 0; form < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(forms); form += 1)
   255:         {
   256:             String8 output = buster_test_temporary_path(arena,
   257:                 string_format(arena, S8("buster-local-sizeof-static-assert-{u32}-{u32}"), case_index, form), S8(".o"));
   258:             String8 syntax_command[] = {S8("-nostdinc"), S8("-g0"), S8("-std=gnu23"), forms[form], S8("-fsyntax-only"), input};
   259:             String8 object_command[] = {S8("-nostdinc"), S8("-g0"), S8("-std=gnu23"), forms[form], S8("-c"), S8("-o"), output, input};
   260:             CompilerDriverInvocation syntax_invocation = compiler_driver_parse_arguments(
   261:                 arena, (SliceString8)BUSTER_ARRAY_TO_SLICE(syntax_command));
   262:             CompilerDriverInvocation object_invocation = compiler_driver_parse_arguments(
   263:                 arena, (SliceString8)BUSTER_ARRAY_TO_SLICE(object_command));
   264:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, syntax_invocation.error == COMPILER_DRIVER_ERROR_NONE);
   265:             BUSTER_TEST(arguments, object_invocation.error == COMPILER_DRIVER_ERROR_NONE);
   266: #if BUSTER_BENCH_ALLOCATIONS
   267:             IrConstructionCounters before = ir_construction_counters();
   268: #endif
   269:             CompilerDriverResult syntax = compiler_driver_execute_invocation(arena, syntax_invocation);
   270: #if BUSTER_BENCH_ALLOCATIONS

  2525:             "// GNU vector_size keeps the requested logical lane count but rounds the\n"
  2526:             "// object image to the next power of two. This fixture crosses every x86-64\n"
  2527:             "// call shape affected by that distinction: scalarized Win64 lanes, native\n"
  2528:             "// AVX/AVX-512 registers, System V memory arguments, three-part integer and\n"
  2529:             "// floating results, and over-aligned hidden-pointer results. The driver also\n"
  2530:             "// builds provider and consumer with different compilers in both directions.\n"

  2723: // GNU explicit casts reinterpret vector bits, including LLVM's add_epi32/64
  2724: // shapes. Keep the source inline because the retirement fixture set is frozen.
  2725: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult compiler_driver_test_bit_count_signatures(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
  2726: {
  2727:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
  2728:     // Keep the source inline: a new tests/ subject would need a separate
  2729:     // native-retirement corpus identity. Foreign targets are object checks;
  2730:     // only the host-native executable below supplies runtime evidence.
  2731:     String8 source = S8(
  2732:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_clz(1u)) == sizeof(int), \"clz int\");\n"
  2733:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_clzl(1ul)) == sizeof(int), \"clzl int\");\n"
  2734:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_clzll(1ull)) == sizeof(int), \"clzll int\");\n"
  2735:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_ctz(1u)) == sizeof(int), \"ctz int\");\n"
  2736:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_ctzl(1ul)) == sizeof(int), \"ctzl int\");\n"
  2737:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_ctzll(1ull)) == sizeof(int), \"ctzll int\");\n"
  2738:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_popcount(1u)) == sizeof(int), \"popcount int\");\n"
  2739:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_popcountl(1ul)) == sizeof(int), \"popcountl int\");\n"
  2740:         "_Static_assert(sizeof(__builtin_popcountll(1ull)) == sizeof(int), \"popcountll int\");\n"
  2741:         "static int calls;\n"
  2742:         "static unsigned long long next(void) { calls += 1; return 0x100000001ull; }\n"
  2743:         "static int count(unsigned long long value) { return __builtin_popcount(value); }\n"
  2744:         "static int leading(unsigned long long value) { return __builtin_clzll(value); }\n"
  2745:         "int main(void)\n"
  2746:         "{\n"
  2747:         "    unsigned long long wide = 0x100000001ull;\n"
  2748:         "    unsigned long long high = (1ull << 40) | 2ull;\n"
  2749:         "    unsigned long long all = ~0ull;\n"
  2750:         "    unsigned char narrow = 1;\n"
  2751:         "    int local = __builtin_popcount(wide);\n"
  2752:         "    int bad = local != 1 || count(wide) != 1;\n"
  2753:         "    bad |= __builtin_popcountl(wide) != (sizeof(long) == 8 ? 2 : 1);\n"
  2754:         "    bad |= __builtin_popcountll(wide) != 2 || __builtin_popcount(1u) != 1;\n"
  2755:         "    bad |= __builtin_popcount(all) != 32;\n"
  2756:         "    bad |= __builtin_popcountll(-1) != 64;\n"
  2757:         "    bad |= __builtin_popcount(3.5) != 2;\n"
  2758:         "    bad |= __builtin_clz(wide) != 31 || __builtin_clzl(high) != (sizeof(long) == 8 ? 23 : 30);\n"
  2759:         "    bad |= __builtin_clzll(1ull) != 63 || leading(1ull) != 63;\n"
  2760:         "    bad |= __builtin_clz(narrow) != 31 || __builtin_clz(1.5) != 31;\n"
  2761:         "    bad |= __builtin_ctz(8u) != 3 || __builtin_ctzl(high) != 1;\n"

  1608: // A bit-field width written as one literal token that is not all decimal
  1609: // digits, or a bit-field whose declarator is parenthesized, used to fail the
  1610: // member segment and drop the whole aggregate. The static assertions check the
  1611: // parse-time layout and the program checks the lowered one; the values match
  1612: // clang 18.
  1613: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL UnitTestResult c_test_bit_field_width_spellings(UnitTestArguments* arguments)
  1614: {
  1615:     UnitTestResult result = {0};
  1616:     String8 source = S8("struct spelled_bits { int a : 0x3; unsigned b : 3u; int c : 0b11; unsigned d : 1'0;\n"
  1617:                         "    int (x) : 3U; unsigned (y) : 0x20; unsigned w : 010; };\n"
  1618:                         "_Static_assert(sizeof(struct spelled_bits) == 12, \"size\");\n"
  1619:                         "_Static_assert(_Alignof(struct spelled_bits) == 4, \"alignment\");\n"
  1620:                         "static struct spelled_bits object;\n"
  1621:                         "int main(void) {\n"
  1622:                         "    object.a = -2; object.b = 7; object.c = 3; object.d = 1023;\n"
  1623:                         "    object.x = -4; object.y = 0xffffffffu; object.w = 0x3ff;\n"
  1624:                         "    if (object.a != -2 || object.b != 7 || object.c != 3 || object.d != 1023) return 1;\n"
  1625:                         "    if (object.x != -4 || object.y != 0xffffffffu || object.w != 255) return 2;\n"
  1626:                         "    return 0;\n"
  1627:                         "}\n");
  1628:     CPreprocessOptions options = {
  1629:         .target = target_native,
  1630:         .data_layout = target_data_layout(target_native),
  1631:         .dialect = C_PREPROCESS_DIALECT_GNU23,
